&+\frac{i}{32}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{k}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\delta}\varepsilon{}^{i l}(\Gamma{}^{b}){}_{\delta}{}^{\rho}\big(\nabla_{b}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\rho}\big)\delta{}^{j}{}_{l}\chi{}_{\beta k} -\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\left.\mathcal R(J){}_{a \gamma i}{}^{j k}\right|\Phi{}^{\gamma}{}_{\beta j k}\\
&-\frac{1}{12}W^{-1}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\left.\mathcal R(J){}_{a}{}^{\beta}{}_{i j}{}^{j}\right|D +\frac{3}{128}W^{-1}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha \beta}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\left.\mathcal R(J){}_{a \gamma i j}{}^{j}\right|(\Gamma{}^{b}){}^{\gamma \delta}\big(\nabla_{b}w{}_{\beta \delta}\big)\\
